| REQ-SRC-ATL105-PDF-001:479 | BR-84-3 | DIRECT_SEGMENT_111 |  | SCENARIO_REVIEW_REQUIRED | MATCHED_ELEMENT_AND_SEMANTICS | Confirmed business-equivalent match: shared element(s) [111], compatible AI/Test Solution segment context [111], no fixed-value conflict, and semantic evidence score 0.385. | BR-SEG100-APPI-VARIABLE-TRIGGER | A Segment 100 transaction requiring Variable Information shall include Segment 111 in Data Section 3 and Element 63 shall include it in the serialized count. | 111 | Bill payment/recurring and installment payment transactions must be identified by a Variable Information Indicator located in the Variable Information section of the Variable Information Data Segment (Segment No. 111). |
| REQ-SRC-ATL105-PDF-001:480 | BR-84-4 | SEGMENT_111_FIELD_RULE |  | SCENARIO_REVIEW_REQUIRED | POTENTIAL_MATCH_REVIEW_REQUIRED | Business-equivalence review required: shared element(s) [113], AI segments [], compared segment [111], Test Solution segments [111], fixed-value conflict [False]. | BR-SEG100-APPI-PURCHASE-RESTRICTION | Sub Table 18 (Purchase Restriction Method) is mandatory on MasterCard Enhanced EMV Fleet (Product IDs MCF/MGF) Authorization, Pre-Authorization, and Completion transactions, and must not be sent for any other transaction; if omitted, Buypass defaults to Visa No Restrictions supported. | 113 | RFID processing requires the appropriate values be sent in Point-of-Sale Entry Mode (contained in Element No. 113, Variable Information) on all transactions when an RFID receiver is connected to the point-of-sale device or controller. |

## AI Requirements Requiring Test Solution Review

Every AI requirement without a confirmed Test Solution match is a red-flag candidate and remains REVIEW_REQUIRED. Validate its source evidence, semantic meaning, scope, scenario linkage, and testability; then map it to an existing Test Solution BR or add a new Test Solution BR only if the validation confirms it is valid. ## Test Solution BRs Without a Confirmed AI Match

| Test Solution BR | Source file | Requirement |
|---|---|---|
| BR-SEG100-APPI-CONTEXT | appendix-i-segment-100-coverage.json | Variable Information data shall be consistent with Segment 100 Prompt Code, card type, entry mode, transaction flow, and merchant configuration. |
| BR-SEG100-APPI-COUNTRY-SUBDIVISION | appendix-i-segment-100-coverage.json | When Terminal Identifier uses the Appendix D non-US/non-Canada state-code condition, Variable Information Table 041 shall carry the country subdivision data required by the source. |
| BR-SEG100-APPI-TABLE-ID | appendix-i-segment-100-coverage.json | Variable Information Segment 111 shall use the Table ID defined for the applicable transaction/card/merchant context. |
| BR-SEG111-MAX-LENGTH | segment-111-core-structure-package.json | The Variable Information Data Segment has a maximum length of 999 alphanumeric characters. |
| BR-SEG111-REPETITION-SEPARATOR | segment-111-core-structure-package.json | Field Separators do not appear between elements within a Variable Information Section repetition or between repetitions; a single Field Separator follows the final repetition. |
